country_credit_rating = {
	set_variable = {
		name = country_credit_note_fixe
		value = country_credit_pondered_mean
	}
	if = {
		limit  = {
			#var:country_credit_note_fixe = 10
			var:country_credit_note_fixe >= 9
			not  = {
				has_modifier = economic_instability
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_AAA
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit  = {
			var:country_credit_note_fixe < 9
			var:country_credit_note_fixe >= 8
			not  = {
				has_modifier = economic_instability
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_AA
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit  = {
			var:country_credit_note_fixe < 8
			var:country_credit_note_fixe >= 7
			not  = {
				has_modifier = economic_instability
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_A
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit  = {
			var:country_credit_note_fixe < 7
			var:country_credit_note_fixe >= 6
			not  = {
				has_modifier = economic_instability
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_BBB
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit  = {
			var:country_credit_note_fixe < 6
			var:country_credit_note_fixe >= 5
			not  = {
				has_modifier = economic_instability
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_BB
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit  = {
			var:country_credit_note_fixe < 5
			var:country_credit_note_fixe >= 4
			not  = {
				has_modifier = economic_instability
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_B
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit  = {
			var:country_credit_note_fixe < 4
			var:country_credit_note_fixe >= 3
			not  = {
				has_modifier = economic_instability
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_CCC
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit  = {
			var:country_credit_note_fixe < 3
			var:country_credit_note_fixe >= 2
			not  = {
				has_modifier = economic_instability
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_CC
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit  = {
			var:country_credit_note_fixe < 2
			var:country_credit_note_fixe >= 1
			not  = {
				has_modifier = economic_instability
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_C
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit  = {
			var:country_credit_note_fixe < 1
			var:country_credit_note_fixe >= 0
			not  = {
				has_modifier = economic_instability
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_D
		}
	}

	#clean country_credit_rating
	if = {
		limit  = {
			var:economic_instability_count >= 2
			or = {
				var:country_bankruptcy = 1
				var:economic_crisis = 1
				var:currency_crisis = 1
				var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 1
				var:financial_crash = 1
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
	}

	#############################################################################
	#exit_from_crisis -> réinitialise les compteur

	#declared_bankruptcy
	if = {
		limit  = {
			#var:economic_instability_count >= 2
			var:country_bankruptcy = 1 #country_bankruptcy & central_bank_bankruptcy n'ont pas d'avertissement il déclanche directement la crise donc ils doivent etre reset automatiquement aprs avoir perdu le modificateur
			not = {
				has_modifier = declared_bankruptcy
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = country_bankruptcy
			value = 0
		}
		economic_instability_count_reset = yes
	}
	#currency_crisis_country
	if = {
		limit  = {
			var:economic_instability_count >= 2
			var:currency_crisis = 1
			not = {
				has_modifier = currency_crisis_country
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = currency_crisis
			value = 0
		}
		economic_instability_count_reset = yes
	}
	#central_bank_bankruptcy_country
	if = {
		limit  = {
			#var:economic_instability_count >= 2
			var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 1
			not = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_bankruptcy_country
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = central_bank_bankruptcy
			value = 0
		}
		economic_instability_count_reset = yes
	}
	#financial_crash_country
	if = {
		limit  = {
			var:economic_instability_count >= 2
			var:financial_crash = 1
			not = {
				has_modifier = financial_crash_country
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = financial_crash
			value = 0
		}
		economic_instability_count_reset = yes
	}
	#economic_crisis_country
	if = {
		limit  = {
			var:economic_instability_count >= 2
			var:economic_crisis = 1
			not = {
				has_modifier = economic_crisis_country
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = economic_crisis
			value = 0
		}
		economic_instability_count_reset = yes
	}

	############################################################################

	#economic_instability
	# -> attribu les notation en fonction des crise en court
	# -> normalement si un pays sort d'une crise (fin des moddifier) -> les compteur sont réinitialiser avant d'ariver içi
	if = {
		limit  = {
			has_modifier = declared_bankruptcy
			var:country_bankruptcy = 1
			not = {
				var:economic_crisis = 1
				var:currency_crisis = 1
				var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 1
				var:financial_crash = 1
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_C #tant que le  modificateur déclarre  bankrrupsi n'est pas enlevé
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit  = {
			has_modifier = economic_instability
			var:economic_crisis = 1
			not = {
				var:currency_crisis = 1
				var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 1
				var:financial_crash = 1
				var:country_bankruptcy = 1
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_CC
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit  = {
			has_modifier = economic_instability
			var:financial_crash = 1
			not = {
				var:currency_crisis = 1
				var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 1
				var:economic_crisis = 1
				var:country_bankruptcy = 1
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_CC
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit  = { #country_credit_rating_D notation D deja attribué pour 23 mois pas de renotation entre temps
			has_modifier = economic_instability
			or = {
				var:currency_crisis = 1
				var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 1
			}
			not = {
				var:financial_crash = 1
				var:economic_crisis = 1
				var:country_bankruptcy = 1
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_D
			months = 12 #à verif lors d'une currency crisisi
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit  = {
			has_modifier = economic_instability
			interconnected_crises > 1
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
		add_modifier = {
			name = country_credit_rating_D
			months = 12 #à verif lors d'une currency crisisi
		}
	}

	#economic_instability reset by year if nothing appen in year #probleme car si reset chaque anné peut plus y avoir de crise donc voir pour abaissé de 0.5/an
	if = {
		limit  = {
			var:economic_instability_count >= 1
			var:economic_crisis = 0
			var:financial_crash = 0
			var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 0
			var:currency_crisis = 0
			#var:country_bankruptcy = 0
			not = {
				has_modifier = declared_bankruptcy
				has_modifier = currency_crisis_country
				has_modifier = central_bank_bankruptcy_country
				has_modifier = financial_crash_country
				has_modifier = economic_crisis_country
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = economic_instability_count
			value = 0
		}
	}
}
sovereign_bond_yields = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = target_interest_rate
			value = { 0.04 0.05 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = target_interest_rate
			value = { 0.05 0.06 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = target_interest_rate
			value = { 0.06 0.07 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = target_interest_rate
			value = { 0.07 0.08 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = target_interest_rate
			value = { 0.08 0.09 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = target_interest_rate
			value = { 0.09 0.1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = target_interest_rate
			value = { 0.10 0.11 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = target_interest_rate
			value = { 0.11 0.12 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = target_interest_rate
			value = { 0.12 0.13 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = target_interest_rate
			value = { 0.13 0.15 }
		}
	}

	#test
	else_if = {
		limit  = {
			not = {
				country_credit_rating_national_debt_compared_gdp_malus_100_on_interest_rate = 1
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = target_interest_rate
			multiply = country_credit_rating_national_debt_compared_gdp_malus_100_on_interest_rate
		}
	}
}

# В5.4 (5.10): the interest on the treasury's loan from its OWN central bank (E&F's "credit at the central bank":
# the player's button, zz_ef_cb_loan, and ai_credit_at_central_bank) is E&F's modifier interest_at_the_central_bank,
# country_expenses_add x the multiplier, a week -- the budget's "additional expenses". The money model took the
# additional income - expenses whole as primary income with abroad (zz_ef_f_aint, the bridge's g_aint): a payment to
# our own CB went into the balance of payments and the clearing. The multiplier is not readable from script, so it is
# kept in var:zz_ef_cbl_int when the modifier is set; the bridge takes it out of the abroad line and shows it as
# treasury -> CB (tools/regen_ef_money_supply_loc.py, zz_ef_v_cbl_int).
# !! MAINTENANCE !! Key-level REPLACE_OR_CREATE of E&F's two effects (01_financial_scripted_effects.txt): the bodies are
# E&F's as of 04.07.2026 plus the variable.
credit_at_central_bank_interest_per_week = {
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = interest_at_the_central_bank } remove_modifier = interest_at_the_central_bank }
	set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cbl_int value = credit_at_central_bank_interest_per_month_by_inflation }
	add_modifier = {
		name = interest_at_the_central_bank
		multiplier = var:zz_ef_cbl_int
	}
}
credit_at_central_bank_interest_per_week_off = {
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = interest_at_the_central_bank } remove_modifier = interest_at_the_central_bank }
	remove_variable = zz_ef_cbl_int
}
ai_credit_at_central_bank = {
	if  = {
		limit = { #pays avec tresorerie faible or de la guerre donc strategie d'endettement constant et roulement de la dette
			credit_at_central_bank_interest_per_week_by_inflation < part_budget_authorized_debt_interest
			national_debt_compared_gdp < national_debt_allowed_relative_gdp
			gold_reserve_compared_gold_reserves_limit < 0.1
			is_at_war = no
		}
		# R2, step 8 (Д.R2.8): the CB's loan to the treasury -- credit_at_central_bank is the ledger's account, the
		# treasury's money is ours (marked); the amount is fixed first (it reads the treasury)
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_t_cbl value = national_debt_allowed_in_gold }
		zz_ef_eng_add_treasury = { V = var:zz_ef_t_cbl }
		every_scope_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = financial_center_historic_place
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_bond_var_state_1
					add = owner.bond_producted
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = government_loan
			add = national_debt_allowed_in_currency
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = credit_at_central_bank
			add = var:zz_ef_t_cbl
		}
		remove_variable = zz_ef_t_cbl
	}
	if = {
		limit  = { #pays en guerre avec reserve inf à 25%, a bessoin d'un coup de mains equivalent la dif pour atteindre 25% + 25% -> pas de limite donc possibilité de faire des 100ain% de dette
			gold_reserve_compared_gold_reserves_limit < 0.15
			is_at_war = yes
		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_t_cbl value = national_debt_allowed_in_gold_war }
		zz_ef_eng_add_treasury = { V = var:zz_ef_t_cbl }
		every_scope_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = financial_center_historic_place
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_bond_var_state_1
					add = owner.bond_producted_war
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = government_loan
			add = national_debt_allowed_in_currency_war
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = credit_at_central_bank
			add = var:zz_ef_t_cbl
		}
		remove_variable = zz_ef_t_cbl
	}
}
ai_refund_central_bank = {
	if  = {
		limit  = {
			gold_reserves_var > 0
			national_debt_unauthorized < gold_reserves_var
			var:credit_at_central_bank > national_debt_allowed_in_gold
			is_at_war = no
		}
		# R2, step 8: the repayment -- the treasury -> the CB's loan (marked); the amount is fixed first
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_t_cbl value = national_debt_unauthorized }
		zz_ef_eng_sub_treasury = { V = var:zz_ef_t_cbl }
		change_variable = {
			name = government_loan
			subtract = var:zz_ef_t_cbl
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:government_loan < 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = government_loan
				value = 0
			}
		}

		change_variable = {
			name = credit_at_central_bank
			subtract = var:zz_ef_t_cbl
		}
		remove_variable = zz_ef_t_cbl
	}
	if  = {
		limit  = {
			credit_at_central_bank_interest_per_week_by_inflation > part_budget_authorized_debt_interest
			national_debt_refund_more_25_at_refund > 0
			is_at_war = no
		}
		# add_treasury = {
		# 	subtract = national_debt_unauthorized #ai_cheat ?
		# }
		# R2, step 8: E&F forgives this part with no money -- the CB writes it off (its loss, var:zz_ef_cb_writeoff)
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_t_cbl value = national_debt_refund_more_25_at_refund }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = zz_ef_cb_writeoff } } set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cb_writeoff value = 0 } }
		zz_ef_post = { FROM = credit_at_central_bank TO = zz_ef_cb_writeoff V = var:zz_ef_t_cbl }
		change_variable = {
			name = government_loan
			subtract = var:zz_ef_t_cbl
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:government_loan < 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = government_loan
				value = 0
			}
		}

		remove_variable = zz_ef_t_cbl
	}
}

ai_buy_bond_1 = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

    #random_in_global_list = {
    #    variable = central_bank_debt_variable_list_ordered_1
    #    save_scope_as = seller
    #}

	random_country = {
		limit = {
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes #has bc
			has_financial_center = yes #has fc
			remaining_debt > 0
			zz_ef_bl_room > 0
			market_owner_is_root_4 = yes
			central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent < 0.75
			not = {
				has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = seller
	}

    if = {
        limit = {
            scope:buyer.var:ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_1 = 0
            exists = scope:seller
            scope:seller = {
                not = {
                    this = root
                }
            }
            gold_reserves_var > gold_reserves_10 #reserve sup à 10% de la reserve
			#bond_quantity_scripted >  0
        }

        random_list = {
            1 = { #5Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_buyer_country_1
                            target = prev
                        }
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_1
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

						#seller
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_1
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_1
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_1
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_1
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_1
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_1
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_1 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_1 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_1
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_1
                            multiply = 5
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_1
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_1
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_1
                        }
                        # R2, step 7: the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_1 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_1
                            value = 5
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_1_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_1_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_1
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
            1 = { #10Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_buyer_country_1
                            target = prev
                        }
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_1
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_1
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_1
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_1
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_1
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_1
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_1 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_1 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_1
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_1
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_1
                            multiply = 10
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_1
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_1
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_1
                        }
                        # R2, step 7: the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_1 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_1
                            value = 10
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_1_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_1_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_1
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
ai_buy_bond_2 = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

    #random_in_global_list = {
    #    variable = central_bank_debt_variable_list_ordered_1
    #    save_scope_as = seller
    #}

	random_country = {
		limit = {
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes #has bc
			has_financial_center = yes #has fc
			remaining_debt > 0
			zz_ef_bl_room > 0
			market_owner_is_root_4 = yes
			central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent < 0.75
			not = {
				has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = seller
	}

    if = {
        limit = {
            scope:buyer.var:ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_2 = 0
            exists = scope:seller
            scope:seller = {
                not = {
                    this = root
                }
            }
            gold_reserves_var > gold_reserves_10 #reserve sup à 10% de la reserve
			#bond_quantity_scripted >  0
        }

        random_list = {
            1 = { #5Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_buyer_country_2
                            target = prev
                        }
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_2
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

						#seller
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_2
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_2
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_2
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_2
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_2
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_2
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_2
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_2
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_2 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_2
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_2
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_2
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_2 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_2
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_2
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_2
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_2
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_2
                            multiply = 5
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_2
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_2
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_2
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_2
                        }
                        # R2, step 7: the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_2 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_2
                            value = 5
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_2_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_2_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_2
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
            1 = { #10Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_buyer_country_2
                            target = prev
                        }
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_2
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_2
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_2
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_2
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_2
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_2
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_2
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_2
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_2 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_2
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_2
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_2
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_2 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_2
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_2
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_2
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_2
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_2
                            multiply = 10
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_2
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_2
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_2
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_2
                        }
                        # R2, step 7: the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_2 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_2
                            value = 10
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_2_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_2_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_2
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_4_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_4_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_4
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
            1 = { #10Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_buyer_country_4
                            target = prev
                        }
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_4
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_4
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_4
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_4
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_4
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_4
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_4
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_4
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_4 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_4
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_4 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_4
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_4
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_4
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_4
                            multiply = 10
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_4
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_4
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_4
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_4
                        }
                        # R2, step 7: the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_4 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_4
                            value = 10
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_4_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_4_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_4
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
ai_buy_bond_5 = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

    #random_in_global_list = {
    #    variable = central_bank_debt_variable_list_ordered_1
    #    save_scope_as = seller
    #}

	random_country = {
		limit = {
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes #has bc
			has_financial_center = yes #has fc
			remaining_debt > 0
			zz_ef_bl_room > 0
			market_owner_is_root_4 = yes
			central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent < 0.75
			not = {
				has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = seller
	}

    if = {
        limit = {
            scope:buyer.var:ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_5 = 0
            exists = scope:seller
            scope:seller = {
                not = {
                    this = root
                }
            }
            gold_reserves_var > gold_reserves_10 #reserve sup à 10% de la reserve
			#bond_quantity_scripted >  0
        }

        random_list = {
            1 = { #5Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_buyer_country_5
                            target = prev
                        }
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_5
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

						#seller
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_5
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_5
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_5
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_5
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_5
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_5
                            value = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #valeur de la quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_5
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_5
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_5 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_total_bond_value_5
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }

                        #interet total
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_5
                            value = 1
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_5
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_5 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_5
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_5
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_5Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_5
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_5
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_5
                            multiply = 5
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_5
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_5
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_5
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_5
                        }
                        # R2, step 7: the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_5 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_5
                            value = 5
                        }

                        #demare le on_action
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_buy_bond_5_on
                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #notification
                    every_country = {
                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_5_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_5
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
            1 = { #10Years
                scope:seller = {
                    scope:buyer = {
                        # Pour les liste du vendeur
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_buyer_country_5
                            target = prev
                        }
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_5
                            target = scope:seller #prev
                        }

                       #seller save scope
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_seller_country_general_5
                            value = scope:seller
                        }

                        ######
                        #quantité
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_bond_quantity_5
                            value = bond_quantity_scripted
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_quantity_purchasing_5
                            value = var:ai_bond_quantity_5
                        }

                        #interret lors de l'achat
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_purchasing_5

                            name = ai_interest_calculated_5
                            value = 1

                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_5
                            multiply = var:ai_bond_quantity_5 #bond quantité
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_5
                            multiply = scope:seller.bond_price
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_calculated_5
                            multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
                        }

                        #interet total à terme
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_5
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_5
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_5
                            multiply = 10
                        }

                        #interet par mois
                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_5
                            value = var:ai_interest_calculated_5
                        }
                        change_variable = {
                            name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_5
                            divide = 12
                        }

                        add_treasury = {
                            subtract = var:ai_total_bond_value_5
                        }
                        # R2, step 7: the treasury's purchase of a foreign bond -- ours (the ledger registers the part)
                        zz_ef_tr_unmark = { V = var:ai_total_bond_value_5 }

                        interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes

                        set_variable = {
                            name = ai_maturity_time_5
                            value = 10
                        }

                        #demare le on_action

                            value = 1
                        }
                    }

                    #notification

                        limit  = {
                            is_player = yes
                        }
                        save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
                        }
                        scope:is_player_for_notification = {
                            post_notification = ai_selle_bond_maturity_5_message
                        }
                    }

                    #################
                    #ajout à la liste des acheteur
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = central_bank_debt_buyer_list_5
                        target = scope:buyer
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
