		save_scope_as = scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25
	}

    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller
			not = {
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_2 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_5 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_6 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_7 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_8 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_11 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_12 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_13 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_14 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_15 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_18 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_19 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_20 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_21 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_24 }
				AND = { exists = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 scope:seller = scope:scope_ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25 }
			}
        }
		scope:seller = {
			scope:buyer = {
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_25
					value = 1 #slot pris
				}

				#selection de la bank
				random_in_list = {
					variable = privat_bank_variable_list_ordered
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}

				# Pour les liste du vendeur
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_25
					target = scope:buyer_privat_bank #prev
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_25
					target = scope:seller #prev
				}

				######
				#quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_25
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_25
					value = 1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_25
					multiply = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_25 #bond quantité
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_25
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}

				#interet total
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_25
					value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_25
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_25
					multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_25
				}
			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_selle_bond_maturity_25_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_buy_bond_maturity_25_message
				}
			}

			#central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes

			#################
			#ajout à la liste des acheteur
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_25 #plusieur bank peuvent etre stack dans cette liste
				target = scope:buyer_privat_bank
			}
		}
    }

#_1 -> à modifier car doit aussi comprendre un netoyage des varriable si uz bank disparée
ai_privat_bank_bond_1_renforcement = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1
		save_scope_as = seller_1
		save_scope_as = seller # bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank and the price read scope:seller (r1008_134657: 'Undefined event target seller')
	}

	#renforcement
    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller_1
			scope:seller_1.remaining_debt > 0
			scope:seller_1.zz_ef_bl_room > 0
        }
		scope:seller_1 = {
			scope:buyer = {
				random_in_list = {
					variable = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_1
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_1
					add = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_1
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_1
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1_new
				}
 			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_1_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_1_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_1
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_1
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_1

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_1
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_1
    }
}

				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_3_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_3_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_3
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_3
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_3

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_3
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_3
    }
}
#_4 -> à modifier car doit aussi comprendre un netoyage des varriable si uz bank disparée
ai_privat_bank_bond_4_renforcement = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4
		save_scope_as = seller_4
		save_scope_as = seller # bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank and the price read scope:seller (r1008_134657: 'Undefined event target seller')
	}

	#renforcement
    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller_4
			scope:seller_4.remaining_debt > 0
			scope:seller_4.zz_ef_bl_room > 0
        }
		scope:seller_4 = {
			scope:buyer = {
				random_in_list = {
					variable = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_4
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_4
					add = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_4
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_4
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4_new
				}
 			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_4_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_4_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_4
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_4
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_4
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_4
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_4
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_4

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_4
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_4
    }
}

		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_9
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_9

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_9
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_9
    }
}
#_10 -> à modifier car doit aussi comprendre un netoyage des varriable si uz bank disparée
ai_privat_bank_bond_10_renforcement = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10
		save_scope_as = seller_10
		save_scope_as = seller # bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank and the price read scope:seller (r1008_134657: 'Undefined event target seller')
	}

	#renforcement
    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller_10
			scope:seller_10.remaining_debt > 0
			scope:seller_10.zz_ef_bl_room > 0
        }
		scope:seller_10 = {
			scope:buyer = {
				random_in_list = {
					variable = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_10
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_10
					add = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_10
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_10
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10_new
				}
 			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_10_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_10_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_10
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_10
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_10
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_10
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_10
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_10

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_10
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_10
    }
}

			scope:seller_16.zz_ef_bl_room > 0
        }
		scope:seller_16 = {
			scope:buyer = {
				random_in_list = {
					variable = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_16
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_16
					add = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_16
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_16
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16_new
				}
 			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_16_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_16_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_16
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_16
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_16
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_16
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_16
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_16

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_16
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_16
    }
}

    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_17
		save_scope_as = seller_17
		save_scope_as = seller # bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank and the price read scope:seller (r1008_134657: 'Undefined event target seller')
	}

	#renforcement
    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller_17
			scope:seller_17.remaining_debt > 0
			scope:seller_17.zz_ef_bl_room > 0
        }
		scope:seller_17 = {
			scope:buyer = {
				random_in_list = {

     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank

					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_17_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_17
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_17_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_17_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_17
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_17

					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_17_new

 			}

				}

			}
			if = {

				}

					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_17_message

 		}
 	}

			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_17
			value = 0
		}

			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_17
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_17

					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_22
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_22
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}
				add_investment_pool = {
					subtract = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22_new
				}
 			}

			#################
			#notification
			every_country = {
				limit  = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = is_player_for_notification
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:seller = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_22_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_22_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_22
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_22
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_22
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_22
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_22
			value = 0
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_22

		clear_variable_list = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_22
		clear_variable_list =  central_bank_debt_privat_bank_buyer_list_22
    }
}
#_23 -> à modifier car doit aussi comprendre un netoyage des varriable si uz bank disparée
ai_privat_bank_bond_23_renforcement = {
    root = {
        save_scope_as = buyer
    }

	random_in_list = {
		variable = ai_privat_bank_seller_country_general_23
		save_scope_as = seller_23
		save_scope_as = seller # bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank and the price read scope:seller (r1008_134657: 'Undefined event target seller')
	}

	#renforcement
    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = scope:seller_23
			scope:seller_23.remaining_debt > 0
			scope:seller_23.zz_ef_bl_room > 0
        }
		scope:seller_23 = {
			scope:buyer = {
				random_in_list = {
					variable = ai_privat_bank_buyer_country_23
     			   	save_scope_as = buyer_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_23
					add = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}

				#valeur de la quantité
				set_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23_new
					value = bond_quantity_scripted_privat_bank
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23_new
					multiply = scope:seller.bond_price_in_gold
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23
					add = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23_new
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23_new > 0
					}
					#interet total -> recalcule total
					set_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_23
						value = var:ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = ai_privat_bank_interest_calculated_23
						multiply = scope:seller.interest_value_10Y
					}
				}

 			}

			if = {

				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_selle_bond_maturity_23_message
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:buyer = scope:is_player_for_notification
				}
				scope:is_player_for_notification = {
					post_notification = ai_privat_bank_renforcement_buy_bond_maturity_23_message
				}
			}

			central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = yes
 		}
 	}

	#clear
    if = {
        limit = {
			not = {
				exists = scope:seller_23
			}
        }
		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_buyer_slot_23
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_quantity_23
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_privat_bank_bond_value_23
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {

			value = 0

# establish bank compagnie
establish_bank_and_ef_compagnie = {

	########################################################################################################################################
	########################################################################################################################################
	########################################################################################################################################
	########################################################################################################################################
	########################################################################################################################################

	# ██████╗  █████╗ ███╗   ██╗██╗  ██╗
	# ██╔══██╗██╔══██╗████╗  ██║██║ ██╔╝
	# ██████╔╝███████║██╔██╗ ██║█████╔╝
	# ██╔══██╗██╔══██║██║╚██╗██║██╔═██╗
	# ██████╔╝██║  ██║██║ ╚████║██║  ██╗
	# ╚═════╝ ╚═╝  ╚═╝╚═╝  ╚═══╝╚═╝  ╚═╝

	########################################################################################################################################
	########################################################################################################################################
	########################################################################################################################################
	########################################################################################################################################
	########################################################################################################################################

	#USA
	#company_BankJPMorgan
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_date > 1871.1.1
			c:USA ?= this
			NOT = {
				has_company = company_type:company_BankJPMorgan
			}
		}
		add_company = company_type:company_BankJPMorgan
		company:company_BankJPMorgan = {
			set_company_state_region = s:STATE_NEW_YORK
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					or = {
						any_scope_building = {
							is_building_type = building_construction_sector
							level < 10
						}
						not = {
							has_building = building_construction_sector
						}
					}
				}
				create_building={
					building="building_construction_sector"
					add_ownership={
						company={
							type=company_BankJPMorgan
							country="c:USA"
							levels=10
						}
					}
					reserves=0
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#company_BankGoldmanSachs
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_date > 1869.1.1
			c:USA ?= this

		}
		random_scope_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					or = {
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector level < 10 }
						not = { has_building = building_construction_sector }
					}
				}
				create_building = {
					building = "building_construction_sector"
					add_ownership = {
						company = {
							type = company_BanqueEgyptienne
							country = "c:EGY"
							levels = 10
						}
					}
					reserves = 0
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# company_BancoNacionalDePanama
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_date > 1904.1.1
			c:PAN ?= this
			NOT = { has_company = company_type:company_BancoNacionalDePanama }
		}
		add_company = company_type:company_BancoNacionalDePanama
		company:company_BancoNacionalDePanama = {
			set_company_state_region = s:STATE_PANAMA
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					or = {
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector level < 10 }
						not = { has_building = building_construction_sector }
					}
				}
				create_building = {
					building = "building_construction_sector"
					add_ownership = {
						company = {
							type = company_BancoNacionalDePanama
							country = "c:PAN"
							levels = 10
						}
					}
					reserves = 0
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#Generic
	###company_private_construction
	if = {
		limit = {
			maximum_number_companies_exceeded_monthly_pulse >= 1 #les Compagnie classique ne +1 pas comme les bank
			NOT = {
				has_company = company_type:company_private_construction
			}
			# any_scope_building = { #inutile il possede deja un centre fiancier
			# 	is_building_type = building_construction_sector
			# 	level >= 1
			# }
		}
		add_company = company_type:company_private_construction
		random_scope_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					or = {
						any_scope_building = {
							is_building_type = building_construction_sector
							level < 10
						}
						not = {
							has_building = building_construction_sector
						}
					}
				}
				create_building={
					building="building_construction_sector"
					add_ownership={
						company={
							type=company_private_construction
							country="ROOT"
							levels=5
						}
					}
					reserves=0
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#EF_historic_compagny

	if = { #NGF - siemens_and_halske
		limit = {
			game_date > 1847
			c:NGF ?= this
			NOT = { has_company = company_type:company_siemens_and_halske }
			#maximum_number_companies_exceeded_monthly_pulse >= 1 #les Compagnie classique ne +1 pas comme les bank
		}
		add_company = company_type:company_siemens_and_halske
		random_scope_state = {
			if = {

				is_company_type = company_type:company_private_construction
				is_company_type = company_type:company_PennsylvaniaRailroad
				is_company_type = company_type:company_standard_oil
			}

				not = {
					has_modifier = UNI_modifier_1
				}
			}
		}
		executive = {
			save_scope_as = company_for_UNI_modifier_1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:company_for_UNI_modifier_1
		}
		scope:company_for_UNI_modifier_1 = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = UNI_modifier_1
				months = 12
			}
		}
	}
}

bond_maturity_1 = {
	#gui timer
	change_variable = {
		name = selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_1
		add = 1
	}

	#################
	if = {
		limit  = {
			var:selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_1 = 4
		}

		every_in_list = {
			variable = seller_country_general_1
			save_scope_as = seller
		}
		post_notification = maturity_arrives_message
	}
	#################

	#maturity
	change_variable = {
		name = selle_bond_maturity_yers_1
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				var:selle_bond_maturity_yers_1 > var:maturity_time_1
				no_longer_exists_1 = 0 #no_longer_exists
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				no_longer_exists_1 = 1
			}
			add_treasury = {
				add = var:total_bond_value_1
			}
		}

		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = buy_bond_1_on
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = interest_purchasing_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = quantity_purchasing_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = interest_calculated_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = total_bond_value_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = interest_per_month_calculated_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = selle_bond_maturity_yers_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = seller_country_general_1
			value = 0
		}

		#seller
		every_in_list = {
			variable = seller_country_general_1
			central_bank_debt_buyer_list_1_clear = yes
			#save_scope_as = seller
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = buyer_country_1

		clear_variable_list = seller_country_general_1
		clear_variable_list = seller_country_general_in_currency_1

		interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes
	}
}

		if = {
			limit = {
				no_longer_exists_3 = 1
			}
			add_treasury = {
				add = var:total_bond_value_3
			}
		}

		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = buy_bond_3_on
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = interest_purchasing_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = quantity_purchasing_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = interest_calculated_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = total_bond_value_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = interest_per_month_calculated_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = selle_bond_maturity_yers_3
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = seller_country_general_3
			value = 0
		}

		#seller
		every_in_list = {
			variable = seller_country_general_3
			central_bank_debt_buyer_list_3_clear = yes
			#save_scope_as = seller
		}

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = buyer_country_3

		clear_variable_list = seller_country_general_3
		clear_variable_list = seller_country_general_in_currency_3

		interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes
	}

ai_bond_maturity_1 = {
	#gui timer
	change_variable = {
		name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_1
		add = 1
	}
	#maturity
	change_variable = {
		name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_1
		add = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				var:ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_1 = var:ai_maturity_time_1
				ai_no_longer_exists_1 = 0 #no_longer_exists
			}
		}

		# В5.5: the payout from nobody removed -- the ledger buys the part back

		#Reset variable
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_buy_bond_1_on
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_interest_purchasing_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_bond_quantity_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_quantity_purchasing_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_time_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_interest_calculated_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_total_bond_value_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_interest_at_term_calculated_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_interest_per_month_calculated_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_selle_bond_maturity_yers_1
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = ai_seller_country_general_1
			value = 0
		}

		#seller
		every_in_list = {
			variable = ai_seller_country_general_1
			central_bank_debt_buyer_list_1_clear = yes
			#save_scope_as = seller
		}
		# scope:seller = {
		# 	central_bank_debt_buyer_list_1_clear = yes
		# }

		#Reset list
		clear_variable_list = ai_buyer_country_1

		clear_variable_list = ai_seller_country_general_1
		interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = yes
	}
}

central_bank_interest_refund_per_year_in_gold = {

	#gold_loaned_to_central_bank reset gold_loaned_to_central_bank
	if = {
		limit = {
			gold_loaned_to_central_bank >= 0
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = gold_loaned_to_central_bank_historic
			subtract = central_bank_interest_refund_per_year
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = gold_loaned_to_central_bank_historic
			multiply = 0.8
		}
	}

	############
	#reset gold_loaned_to_central_bank_historic
	############
	if = {
		limit = {
			gold_loaned_to_central_bank < 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = gold_loaned_to_central_bank_historic #gold_loaned_to_central_bank_historic
			value = 0
		}
	}

	############
	#fixe var
	############
	#gold_loaned_to_central_bank_fix
	set_variable = {
		name = gold_loaned_to_central_bank_fix
		value = gold_loaned_to_central_bank
	}

	#gui -> gold_loaned_to_central_bank_no_interest_fix
	set_variable = {
		name = gold_loaned_to_central_bank_no_interest_fix #-> [Country.MakeScope.Var('gold_loaned_to_central_bank_no_interest_fix').GetValue|D+]
		value = gold_loaned_to_central_bank_no_interest
	}
}
interest_per_month_from_foreign_debt_investment = {
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = interest_from_foreign_debt_investment } remove_modifier = interest_from_foreign_debt_investment }
}

financial_center_production_methods = {
 	every_scope_state = {

        ############################
		#manufacture_stock
		############################ 
		if = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					manufacture_stock_market_goods_sell_orders > 0
					not = {
						or = {
							has_law = law_type:law_cooperative_ownership
							has_law = law_type:law_command_economy
						}
					}
				}
				or = {
					is_production_method_active = {
						building_type = building_financial_centre
						production_method = pm_no_manufacture_stock_exchange
					}

		#building_food_industry

buy_bond_in_currrency_var = {
	every_scope_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site
		}
	}

	scope:seller = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_currency
			}
			scope:buyer = {
				scope:central_bank_site = {
					change_variable = {
						name = stockpiling_dinar_state_1
						subtract = $PBIC_1$
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_algerian_dinar_currency
			}
			scope:buyer = {
				scope:central_bank_site = {
					change_variable = {
						name = stockpiling_dinar_algerian_dinar_state_1
						subtract = $PBIC_1$
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_iraqi_dinar_currency
			}
			scope:buyer = {
				scope:central_bank_site = {
					change_variable = {
						name = stockpiling_dinar_iraqi_dinar_state_1
						subtract = $PBIC_1$
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_libyan_dinar_currency
			}
			scope:buyer = {
				scope:central_bank_site = {
					change_variable = {
						name = stockpiling_dinar_libyan_dinar_state_1
						subtract = $PBIC_1$
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_moroccan_dirham_currency
			}
			scope:buyer = {
				scope:central_bank_site = {
					change_variable = {
						name = stockpiling_dinar_moroccan_dirham_state_1
						subtract = $PBIC_1$
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_omanian_rial_currency
			}
			scope:buyer = {
				scope:central_bank_site = {
					change_variable = {
						name = stockpiling_dinar_omanian_rial_state_1
						subtract = $PBIC_1$
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_qiran_currency
			}
			scope:buyer = {
				scope:central_bank_site = {
					change_variable = {

						subtract = $PBIC_1$
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_saudi_riyal_currency
			}
			scope:buyer = {
				scope:central_bank_site = {
					change_variable = {
						name = stockpiling_dinar_saudi_riyal_state_1
						subtract = $PBIC_1$
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_serbian_dinar_currency

currency_crisis = {
	#objectif:
	# - la devise baisse de 50% -> les pays étranger perde la confiance -> revente massive de la devise -> inflation scripter qui calcule la différence excte de valeur etre les 2 extreme de valeur
	# - revente integrale de la devise possedé par les pays étranger
	# - control des change pour ne pas etre acheter
	# - blocage des route de commerce

	capital= {
		save_scope_as = capital_state
	}
	save_scope_as = target_country

	set_variable = {
		name = currency_crisis
		value = 1
	}
	currency_crisis_count_reset = yes
	country_credit_rating = yes
	economic_instability = yes
}
economic_crisis = {
	capital= {
		save_scope_as = capital_state
	}
	save_scope_as = target_country

	set_variable = {
		name = economic_crisis
		value = 1
	}
	economic_crisis_count_reset = yes
	#country_credit_rating = yes
	economic_instability = yes
} #-> revante devise en mass + pas de rachat si foreign_exchange_controls
financial_crash = {
	capital= {
		save_scope_as = capital_state
	}
	save_scope_as = target_country

	set_variable = {
		name = financial_crash
		value = 1
	}
	financial_crash_count_reset = yes
	#country_credit_rating = yes
	economic_instability = yes
}
central_bank_bankruptcy = {
	capital= {
		save_scope_as = capital_state
	}
	save_scope_as = target_country

	if = {
		limit  = {
			or = {
				var:economic_instability_count = 0
				var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 0
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = money_value_target_1
			divide = 2
		}
		trade_balance = yes #j'actualisa la trade balance direct car des le mois suivant le central_bank_bankruptcy va retrigger si le pats est toujour en extreme weak
	}

	set_variable = {
		name = central_bank_bankruptcy
		value = 1
	}
	central_bank_bankruptcy_count_reset = yes
	#country_credit_rating = yes
	economic_instability = yes
} #-> revante devise en mass + pas de rachat si foreign_exchange_controls

country_bankruptcy = {
	capital= {
		save_scope_as = capital_state
	}
	save_scope_as = target_country

	set_variable = {
		name = country_bankruptcy
		value = 1
	}

	#country_credit_rating = yes
	economic_instability = yes
}
########################################################################################

economic_instability = {

	########################################################################################
	# #test
	# set_variable = {
	# 	name = country_indice_value_dif_01
	# 	value = -0.6
	# }
	########################################################################################

	root = {
		save_scope_as = target_country
	}
	#Country Bankrupt - ok

	#event: ? si déclanche grace au trigger

	#Bank central Bankrupt = weak currency - ok
	#Crise monetaire = currency crisis (diminution de la valeur de la devise de plus de 50%/) -> délock l'inflation -> création d'inflation dans le monetary policy -> crise inflationniste du a la dette à vopitr
	#Crise économique =  Economic crisis (diminution de la valeur du PIB de plus de 50%)
	#Crash financier = Financial crash (diminution de la valeur de l'index des marché de plus de 50%)

		#pour le modifier economic_instability les pays sont vendeur sur la devise meme en dehor d'une crise de la dvise


	##############
	#General
	##############

	#perte de prestige 25% voir plus
	#malus diplomatique avec tout les pays uniquement si default sur dette

	##############
	#evenement spe
	##############

	change_variable = {
		name = economic_instability_count
		add = 1
	}
	economic_instability_count_reset = yes


	#economic_instability concequences
	if = {
		limit  = {
			or  = {
				var:economic_instability_count >= 2
				var:country_bankruptcy >= 1
				#is_at_war = yes #idée que si tu est en guerre alors tu trigger des le premier avertissement
				#var:central_bank_bankruptcy >= 1 #-> Teste division pa 2 de la money value target pour essayer de sauvé le pays avant de déclanché la crise
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit  = {
				var:economic_instability_count = 1 #en cas de country_bankruptcy ou de central_bank_bankruptcy l'évenement economic_instability ce déclanche meme si l'instabilité est que de 1
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = economic_instability_count
				value = 2 #cela bloque l'attribution de note
			}
		}

		#country_bankruptcy
		if = {
			limit  = {
				var:country_bankruptcy >= 1
				not = {
					has_modifier = declared_bankruptcy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
			add_modifier = {
				name = country_credit_rating_C
			}

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = economic_instability } remove_modifier = economic_instability }
			add_modifier = {
				name = economic_instability
				months = 23
			}

			# remove_modifier = modifierr vanila
			# add_modifier = {
			# 	name = modifierr vanila
			# 	months = 23
			# }
		}

		# currency_crisis/central_bank_bankruptcy
		if = {
			limit  = {
				or = {
					var:currency_crisis >= 1
					var:central_bank_bankruptcy >= 1
				}
				not = {
					or = {
						has_modifier = central_bank_bankruptcy_country
						has_modifier = currency_crisis_country
					}
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = foreign_exchange_controls } remove_modifier = foreign_exchange_controls }
			add_modifier = {
				name = foreign_exchange_controls
				months = 23
			}

			#déplacé dans le currency_strength_modifier pour etre sure de déétruire la masse monetaire dans la balance si jamais
			#mais je le laisse ici pour le currency_crisis
			if = {
				limit  = {
					has_modifier = has_central_bank
					not = {
						has_modifier = central_bank_bankruptcy_country
					}
				}
				every_country = {
					if = {
						limit  = {
							has_modifier = has_central_bank
							not = {
								this = root
								#is_subject = yes #à voir car  il faut  que  tout  soit  vendu
							}
						}
						save_scope_as = seller
						all_currency_resold = yes
					}
				}
			}

			#-> création d'hyper inflation scripté

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
			add_modifier = {
				name = country_credit_rating_D
				months = 23
				#-> blocage des route de commerce qui devrai rester grace au extreme weak currency (pas besoins d'un modificateur siupplémentaire)
				#-> nécessité d'atribué le extrem weak currency oui ou non ? -> deja présent pour la bank central bankrupcy car c'est le ex xeak qui le déclanche
			}

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = economic_instability } remove_modifier = economic_instability }
			add_modifier = {
				name = economic_instability
				months = 23
			}
			if = {
				limit  = {
					var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 1
				}
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = central_bank_bankruptcy_country } remove_modifier = central_bank_bankruptcy_country }
				add_modifier = {
					name = central_bank_bankruptcy_country
					months = 23
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit  = {
					var:currency_crisis = 1
				}
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = currency_crisis_country } remove_modifier = currency_crisis_country }
				add_modifier = {
					name = currency_crisis_country
					months = 23
				}
			}

			#passage en fiat currency car plus d'import possible d'or ou d'argznt
			activate_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
			central_bank_production_methods = yes

			#Event for player
			capital= {
				save_scope_as = capital_state
			}
			save_scope_as = target_country

			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_player = yes
				}
				trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.30
			}

		}

		#financial_crash
		if = {
			limit  = {
				var:financial_crash >= 1
				not = {
					has_modifier = financial_crash_country
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit  = {
					# not = {
					# 	is_subject = yes
					# }
					has_modifier = has_central_bank
					has_modifier = has_financial_center
				}
				financial_crash_consequences = yes
			}

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
			add_modifier = {
				name = country_credit_rating_CC
			}

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = economic_instability } remove_modifier = economic_instability }
			add_modifier = {
				name = economic_instability #bloc l'achat de  la devise et favorrise sa vente / bloc le base ratre à 10%
				months = 23
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = financial_crash_country } remove_modifier = financial_crash_country }
			add_modifier = {
				name = financial_crash_country
				multiplier = var:country_indice_value_dif_01
				months = 23
			}
		}

		#economic_crisis
		if = {
			limit  = {
				var:economic_crisis >= 1
				not = {
					has_modifier = economic_crisis_country
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit  = {
					# not = {
					# 	is_subject = yes
					# }
					has_modifier = has_central_bank
					has_modifier = has_financial_center
				}
				economic_crisis_consequences = yes
			}

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AAA }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_AA }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_A } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_A }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BBB }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_BB }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_B } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_B }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CCC }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_CC }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_C } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_C }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D } remove_modifier = country_credit_rating_D }
			add_modifier = {
				name = country_credit_rating_C
			}

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = economic_instability } remove_modifier = economic_instability }
			add_modifier = {
				name = economic_instability #bloc l'achat de  la devise et favorrise sa vente / bloc le base ratre à 10%
				months = 23
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = economic_crisis_country } remove_modifier = economic_crisis_country }
			add_modifier = {
				name = economic_crisis_country
				months = 23
			}
		}

		country_credit_rating = yes
	}
}

financial_crash_consequences = {

	financial_crash_destroy_financial_center = yes

	#perte de capital du privé
	change_variable = {
		name = government_loan
		subtract = investor_massive_capital_loss
	}
	add_investment_pool = investor_massive_capital_loss

	#faillite des entreprise
	bankrupt_company = yes #-> à revoir

	#faillite batiment privé
	every_scope_building = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				#financial center
				is_building_type = building_financial_centre

				is_building_type = building_sulfur_mine
				is_building_type = building_oil_rig

				#railway
				is_building_type = building_railway

			private_ownership_fraction > 0.5
